# Data sources

Raw downloads live in `data/raw/` untouched. One row per file.
Files were renamed on 2026-09-24 (contents unchanged; sha256 re-checked).
Third-party transcriptions live in `data/manual_local/` (gitignored); our own compilations live in `data/manual/` (tracked). Their rows give the full path; all other `file` entries are relative to `data/raw/` (gitignored).

Third-party data must be obtained from the sources listed here; the public repo holds code, our derived results and citations only (publication rule, 2026-09-24).

`publication` column:
- **publish**: our derived results may be published with attribution; the terms would also allow redistributing the data (we still do not commit `data/raw/`).
- **cite-only**: our derived results and cited facts may be published with a citation; the data and our transcriptions of it stay local.
- **internal**: used internally only; nothing derived from it goes on the public site without the owner's written consent.

Values as printed (PD/BI to 2–3 s.f.); HK$ amounts with M/K suffixes expanded to plain HKD (e.g. 23.3M -> 23300000, 9.1K -> 9100). Asset names as printed on p. 80; the heat map on p. 79 calls "Qibao Vanke Plaza" "Link Plaza Qibao". Colour-only heat map (PDF p. 79) not transcribed. AXA assessment done in 2023/24 per p. 79. Moved to data/manual_local/ (gitignored) 2026-09-24, publication rule. | ⚠️ PUBLICATION LIMIT. Link REIT Terms of Use: "These may not be used, reproduced, distributed or published in any medium for any purpose without the prior written permission of us or the party owning them." https://www.linkreit.com/en/terms-of-use-and-disclaimer/ | cite-only |

Original name `copy_of_global_flood_depth-damage_functions__30102017.xlsx`. sha256 5b944b59…ddad. 12 sheets. Sheet "Damage functions": damage class in col A, depth (m) in col B, regional fractions in cols C–I (EUROPE … ASIA = col F … GLOBAL), std dev from col J; commercial buildings rows 13–21 (depth 0–6 m). Values are constants, not formulas. Max damage per country in "MaxDamage-*" sheets (€/m², 2010). | Attribution (verify). No licence text in the workbook; same JRC record (JRC105688) as the report, whose p. 2 notice authorises reuse "provided the source is acknowledged". | publish |

GEO's conditions: the source is clearly acknowledged as "Geotechnical Engineering Office, Civil Engineering and Development Department" or "GEO, CEDD"; the dataset is not used in a manner that implies official endorsement by the GEO or CEDD; no modification is made that could misrepresent its original meaning or context. Our implementation of GEO's conditions (not GEO's wording): that attribution text exactly, on every map layer or chart using HLC and on the sources page; site footer/methodology says "Independent student project; not endorsed by GEO or CEDD.", no GEO/CEDD logos; reprojection (e.g. to EPSG:4326), spatial subsetting to the study area and display styling allowed; changing attribute values, merging/reclassifying records, or presenting our derived landslide score as GEO data not allowed; sources page cites the Terms of Use and its disclaimer. Background: the GEO Data Portal Terms (https://www.ginfo.cedd.gov.hk/geoopendata/eng/TnC.aspx) and the HLC metadata use constraints (prior written consent for publication; no distribution to third parties) conflicted; internal-only from 2026-09-24 until GEO's confirmation. | publish (GEO permission 2026-10-06, conditions) |
